# Agents · V · part 4

1,040 entries, verifier to verifier. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **verifier**: Verifies dnd-app changes in the browser preview and returns a short verdict — console, network, computed styles, real clicks, screenshots when a visual bug is claimed. Knows the SW-cache, WebGL-screenshot and theme traps. Call before closing a phase or a release when UI, CSS or tab behaviour changed; it edits nothing. Триггеры: «проверь в превью», «верификация», «правка не видна». ([D1MANYCH/dnd-app](https://github.com/D1MANYCH/dnd-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Runs a workorder's acceptance criteria against the working tree and reports PASS or IMPL-DEFECT with evidence. Read-only. Use after the implementer returns IMPL-DONE. Judges nothing it cannot execute — subtle correctness is the domain reviewers' job, not this agent's. ([falorfrozen-cmd/hero-siege-offline-toolkit](https://github.com/falorfrozen-cmd/hero-siege-offline-toolkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Validates work by running tests, lint, and checks; reports pass/fail and any failures. Use for verification before considering a task complete. ([HKTITAN/cursor-best-practices](https://github.com/HKTITAN/cursor-best-practices/blob/HEAD/cursor-best-practices/assets/templates/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Separate checker for the verified-work flow. Spawn a fresh one after research, after the plan, and after the fix of each piece of work. It never checks work it did. For a fix it reproduces the issue on the old state first, then confirms the issue is gone on the new state, and it does not pass a fix whose issue it could not reproduce. ([jl-cmd-projects/claude-dev-env](https://github.com/jl-cmd-projects/claude-dev-env/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-dev-env/.agents/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Re-reads synthesized ComfyUI-Koolook PR review findings against the actual codebase, drops false positives, and classifies each Medium+ finding. Accepts batches preferably grouped by cited file. ([malkuthro/ComfyUI-Koolook](https://github.com/malkuthro/ComfyUI-Koolook/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/review-pr-comfy-cursor/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Verification specialist that runs checks and reports evidence before completion claims ([polymetrics-ai/cli](https://github.com/polymetrics-ai/cli/blob/HEAD/.pi/extensions/pi-sub-agent/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Evidence-based claim verifier. Use when a PR, delivery-agent, or commit claims "X now works" / "Y is fixed" / "tests pass" and you need grounded confirmation before merge or before claiming completion. Runs the relevant tests, lints, builds, and status checks and reports exact output + exit codes. NOT for general code review (use code-review) and NOT for writing tests or fixes (use delivery-agent). ([POWERFULMOVES/PMOVES.AI](https://github.com/POWERFULMOVES/PMOVES.AI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Check claims, numbers, dates and sources in a draft or finding against the original source before it reaches the user. Use after research with numbers, before a mail with figures, or when a result looks too clean. ([Relay-inc/relay-app](https://github.com/Relay-inc/relay-app/blob/HEAD/src-tauri/resources/default-vault/System/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Closes one node of a task-pipeline work graph with a hand-written verdict. Reads the node's requirement, the diff and the output of the node's check, and returns the seven-key verdict that graph.py close accepts, with evidence for every done claim, the blockers and a re-plan. Use when a verdict is already in flight or certification does not cover the node. Not for code review, and not instead of the certification tiers. ([ssheleg/task-pipeline](https://github.com/ssheleg/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-pipeline/agents/verifier.md) · ★7)
- **verifier**: Ejecuta las verificaciones y los evals de una tarea o de una spec con el CLI, registra la evidencia y compara con los umbrales de la política y de la suite. Usar proactivamente cuando una tarea pasa a revisión, antes del revisor, y en cada reintento. No edita código ni evidencia, no interpreta los resultados a favor del autor y no aprueba compuertas. ([axellab/harness-framework](https://github.com/axellab/harness-framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic/agents/verifier.md) · ★6 · 6 copies)

Стартует от критериев приёмки из spec.md, а не от кода: сам придумывает проверки, включая враждебные случаи, прогоняет их и доказывает каждый критерий наблюдаемым результатом. Пишет только тесты, код фичи не трогает. ([aza9908/pingwin-skills](https://github.com/aza9908/pingwin-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Valide le travail terminé, exécute les tests et dresse un rapport succès / incomplet. ([Azonesbz/claude-config](https://github.com/Azonesbz/claude-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-methodology/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: The Verifier. Phase 5 of a Ruby build. In a fresh context, runs the dependency rule checker (packwerk, Rails projects only), a SIFT audit, and the acceptance tests, and writes no code. Use for Ruby code quality audits, PR reviews, 'is this production ready' questions, and architecture boundary checks. ([b08x/ruby-dev-plugin](https://github.com/b08x/ruby-dev-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)

- **verifier**: Corre la cadena de verificación de un addon de WoW y devuelve un veredicto CONCISO. Devuelve PASS/FAIL por etapa + SOLO las líneas que fallan. Solo lectura + Bash: NO edita código. Úsalo para mantener la salida verbosa de linters y tests FUERA del hilo principal y ahorrar tokens. ([bettogamer/QuantumDungeonTools](https://github.com/bettogamer/QuantumDungeonTools/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Epic 完整機器驗證後的獨立唯讀驗收者；一次全新上下文驗收，後續只複核修正差異。 ([bibirock/spex](https://github.com/bibirock/spex/blob/HEAD/assets/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Checks a pull request's head in the running app without having seen it built. ([binarieslab/asdlc-blueprint](https://github.com/binarieslab/asdlc-blueprint/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Adversarial QA for Build Harness. Invoke to audit a capability the builder claims is done, to refute or confirm someone else's finding, to re-verify fixes, or to synthesise verified findings into a ranked report. Defaults to "refuted" unless it reproduces the claim itself, fresh. Never fixes code. ([biplob819/build-harness](https://github.com/biplob819/build-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Adversarial verification of completed work. Use PROACTIVELY before reporting multi-file or high-stakes work as done — give it the exact claim being made plus the files/diff involved; it subsumes running the canonical check. For a quick end-to-end drive of a single small change, use the built-in /verify skill instead; never run both. It tries to REFUTE the claim by reading the real code and running the real checks, and returns a verdict backed only by command output. ([blyatiful1/hardmode](https://github.com/blyatiful1/hardmode/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Runs the verification commands for the current change and reports the results without editing anything. - **verifier**: Adversarial test verifier (role V). - **verifier**: Bramka weryfikacji (stop-gate). Używaj PROAKTYWNIE po każdej zmianie kodu, zanim zadanie zostanie zgłoszone jako skończone. Uruchamia testy, linter i ewaluacje, sprawdza, czy opis zmiany zgadza się z diffem. Nie poprawia kodu, tylko wydaje werdykt. - **verifier**: Container do gate de conformidade do SDD (skill verify-sprint): julga se a entrega de um sprint corresponde à definição, sem poder de edição — Read/Grep/Glob para evidência e Bash para re-executar a prova. - **verifier**: Revisor adversarial. - **verifier**: Pre-commit audit specialist. Reads the staged diff and reviews it against the repo standards; returns findings in tiers. ([Busy-Office/ai-skills](https://github.com/Busy-Office/ai-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/loop-atlas/fixtures/crew/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs declared acceptance checks and reports exact evidence without editing implementation files. Use after blocking review findings are resolved. ([ByteBoyy/agentic-dev-orchestrator](https://github.com/ByteBoyy/agentic-dev-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Reviews a story's diff against its acceptance criteria and the conventions, for the agent working the story, reading the story's last flai verify result rather than running the tests, lint, and flai check itself. - **verifier**: Ejecuta pnpm verify (o un subconjunto) y devuelve un resumen compacto de los fallos agrupados por herramienta. - **verifier**: Fast Haiku verifier that tries to refute one concrete claim or finding (a bug, a file:line fact, a check result) against the code. - **verifier**: Fresh-context, independent verification after any other role's work is "done" — executor, ui-designer, tech-writer, security-review. - **verifier**: Evaluador escéptico de UGC Factory — ejecuta la Verificación literal de una tarea de planning.md contra el sistema real levantado, persiste la evidencia en docs/verifications/&lt;ID&gt;/ y emite PASS/FAIL. - **verifier**: Adversarial second reader for river-review. - **verifier**: Evidence-checking seat for the senioro:orchestrate Tech Lead — rules on claims, review findings, or an implementer's self-report by reading the cited code and running the narrowest checks.
